- union - combine graphs
- intersection - take overlaping elements from graphs
# Graph isomorphism
- the function $f$ is called graph isomorphism
- every graph isomorphism preserves adjacency
- every graph isomorphism preserves degrees of vertices

$\{ a,b \}\text{ is mapped to edge}s\{ 1,2 \}$
$\{ b,c \}\text{is mapped to }\{ 1,3 \}$
$\{ 2,3 \}\text{ is an edge in }T_{2}\text{, which is not obtained from an edge in}\Gamma_{1}$

$f:V_{1}\to V_{2}$
$f(V_{1})=V_{2}$     - is a bijection
f is injective

but it is not a graph isomorphism

Is it Eulerian?
No because it has  verteceses of odd degress
Is it Hamiltonian?
No we cannot visit vertex "a" and go back w/o revisiting vertex "b"
Not a tree because it has cycles
but it has Subgraphs which are trees
Adjacency matrix

|     | a   | b   | c   | d   | e   | f   | g   |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| a   |     | 1   |     |     |     |     |     |
| b   | 1   |     | 1   |     | 1   | 1   |     |
| c   |     | 1   |     | 1   |     |     |     |
| d   |     |     | 1   |     | 1   | 1   |     |
| e   |     | 1   |     | 1   |     |     |     |
| f   |     | 1   |     | 1   |     |     | 1   |
| g   |     |     |     |     |     | 1   |     |
Recover graph from adjacency matrix
$\begin{bmatrix} & a & b & c & d & e \\  a & 0 & 1 & 1 & 0 & 0  \\  b & 1 & 0 & 0 & 0 & 0 \\  c & 1 & 0 & 0 & 1 & 1 \\  d & 0 & 0 & 1 & 0 & 0 \\  e & 0 & 0 & 1 & 0 & 0\end{bmatrix}$
